Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the paleobiology object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 940. The moduli space has dimension at most 984 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on paleobiology structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 940. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 984. QED.
